# Skills · F · part 8

963 entries, factory-runbook to fail-fast-enforcement-agent. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **factory-runbook**: Use when a factory run must post its changes — check out the run branch, commit a step's allowlisted paths, push only that branch, and open or reuse one draft MR/PR with milestone/assignee ownership. Also the shared home for factory run contracts (executor, tracker integration, lanes, brief and provider templates). ([tikalk/adlc-team-skills](https://github.com/tikalk/adlc-team-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/factory/factory-runbook) · ★145 · 2 copies)
- **factory-safari-check**: Check a page in real Safari (not Playwright WebKit) from a session — open a URL through safaridriver, read computed styles with a probe script, save a screenshot. Use only when a change renders differently by browser engine and Safari has to be confirmed — scrollbars, `::-webkit-*` pseudo-elements, `@supports` fallbacks, backdrop-filter, form controls, date inputs — or when a plan or PR says "Safari not checked". Triggers on "check it in Safari", "Safari fallback", "does Safari support", "WebKit fallback unchecked". ([albertogodinez/factory-core](https://github.com/albertogodinez/factory-core/tree/HEAD/bundles/core/factory-safari-check) · 2 copies)
- **factory-sales-concept**: Builds an Arabic RTL Next.js digital-concept platform for a factory or manufacturer after inspecting their public website: sales-call copy, WhatsApp QR, human-in-the-loop automation, GitHub repo, and Vercel deploy. Same production pattern as Al-Majjarra and Banoon Kids. Use when starting a factory project under C:\Factories, C:\almajjarra, C:\NoorPlasticFactory, or when the user says مصنع, تصور رقمي, مدير المبيعات, factory-sales-concept, or pastes the factory intake prompt. ([kazoya/rctc-skill](https://github.com/kazoya/rctc-skill/tree/HEAD/factory-sales-concept))
- **factory-sandbox**: Valida el sandbox del provider de una conexión Pull: lanza ~6 endpoints clave en paralelo (auth, search, statics hotels, statics rooms, prebook, book), captura responses y compara contra la doc declarada. Es la Fase 2 de Pull. Registra todo en `actions(action_type='sandbox_validate')`. Invocar cuando Santi diga "valida sandbox de X", "Fase 2 de X", "/factory-sandbox validate X". ([Perlatours/factory](https://github.com/Perlatours/factory/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/factory-sandbox))
- **factory-schedule-sync**: Build a high-leverage agenda for an alignment/sync meeting. Looks at recent changes, the backlog, and issues that lack context or alignment, cross-references who's attending against ownership, and proposes a discussion list aimed at decisions and priorities — not status theater. Collaborative: you refine the agenda together, and refining it surfaces context worth writing back to issues. Reads per-project rules from `factory/`. Use when the user runs /factory-schedule-sync, says "prep for the sync", "build the meeting agenda", or "what should we talk about". - **factory-security**: Security conventions for builds that touch sensitive data, regulated industries, or AI-generated code paths. Covers KMS encryption at rest, BAA verification for PHI in email/SMS, safe URL redirects, admin-client bypass guardrails, in-memory rate-limiter caveats, the "read-only by default" stance for AI-generated code, mandatory-review-queue pattern, and request tracing for support workflows. - **factory-security**: The Team Leader seat's security read on a PR, run as part of its audit BEFORE the human merges. Explicit-invoke: run when a PR touches credentials, workflows, auth, payments, or personal data — or when the human asks 'is this safe to merge?'. Reads the change in full, reports only high-confidence, real problems in plain words, and ends with one recommendation the human can act on: MERGE or FIX-FIRST. No security theater — no theoretical noise, no best-practice lint. This is a Team Leader audit capability, not a separate seat: security is a skill, and the roster stays four. Never merges, never… ([squidbay/factory](https://github.com/squidbay/factory/tree/HEAD/skills/factory-security) · ★5)
- **factory-security-engineer**: Use to threat-model a feature, audit AI-generated code, design sensitive-data handling, or review auth/authz boundaries. Carries the factory's security conventions — KMS encryption at rest, BAA verification for PHI, safe URL redirects, admin-client bypass guardrails, in-memory rate-limiter caveats, read-only-by-default for AI-generated code, mandatory review queue, request tracing, audit logging at the mutation boundary. - **factory-stacked-pr**: Create, maintain, and land a stack of dependent pull requests. Use GitHub's `gh stack` extension where the host supports it; otherwise chain base branches by hand.
